- **Completed:** Alpha 15 set, package **WP-16 DELIVERED** (merge `23e3c954`, main-first). Closes the KL-159 round-4 corner: an empty directory record in a `.knxproj` (outer archive and protected nested payload, same check) is refused by name as `InconsistentRecord` when its CRC-32 is not 0, or when it is compressed without a complete stream (no bytes with any method but stored, a truncated deflate stream, bytes that are no deflate stream, a stream that inflates to bytes, bytes behind the stream). The refusal happens while the container opens, before anything is parsed or stored, so existing project and product databases stay byte-for-byte unchanged. Still accepted: stored empty directories (ETS6, `jar` with UTF-8 flag and `0xCAFE` extra), Java `ZipOutputStream`'s deflated `03 00` with bit 3 and a signed descriptor, and any complete empty deflate stream. A census of the private corpus (231 archives, 222 directory records) refuses none of them. Code: `crates/knx-etsproj/src/container/local_record.rs` (`check_directory` replaces `directory_carries_data`), `container.rs`; tests: 9 in `decoded_member_names`, 1 in `knx-app` `import_service` (seeded project and product DB files/WALs compared after both refusals). Docs: KL-159 dated section (retained block untouched), OPEN_WORK WP-16, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS. Acceptance: cross-vendor review (Codex `gpt-6.1-sol`, high) accepted without findings; change-scoped package gates green on `348500c3` and on the rebased head (Rust 2,367 passed / 0 failed / 175 ignored in 27 binaries, private corpus 149/149 selected; the full corpus run on the branch was 155/155 in 9 targets); 6 mutants killed (CRC check, empty data for any method, incomplete stream reason, other method with no bytes, `total_in`, `total_out`).
